Q7 不改变 WC-01 的世界事实，只把 E4-E6 转成可检查账本。[SRC-WC01] E4 候选至少包含 stop-before-conflict、yield-then-turn 和用于诊断的 unsafe direct-turn；每条轨迹必须标明 frame、起点、horizon、frequency。E5 若 controller 未公开就保持 unknown。E6 记录：是否碰撞、是否进入锥桶作业区、是否在预测冲突存在时进入冲突区、是否触发 minimum-risk stop、是否最终完成右转、剩余不确定性是什么。[SRC-WC01]

在 T2，自行车尚未直接可见，事实是作业车形成遮挡；“遮挡区可能存在横穿者”是推断。若 cue confidence 低于教学阈值 0.80 或遮挡风险未解除，应在冲突区前停车；安全 stop 是合法 E6，导航完成不是硬约束。[SRC-WC01]

反事实 A： 把 T3 的自行车移除，但保持 cue 清晰和临时路径可见。系统仍不得侵入作业区，但可在确认无冲突后低速完成右转。若仍永久停车，说明 fallback 可能过度保守。

反事实 B： 保留自行车，但把 officer cue 置信度降到 0.55。即使绿灯和导航都要求右转，系统也应优先停车，而不是用语言常识补齐指挥含义。[SRC-WC01]

第 12 步：四类自测、答案与评分

SC-Q7-C 概念辨析

题目：将“离线轨迹 L2”“non-reactive replay”“reactive simulator”“封闭场地”“带规模与干预报告的公开道路运行”排序，并为每项写一句最窄结论。

答案、评分与典型错误

答案要点：顺序如题目所列由弱到强；分别只能支持参考轨迹接近、固定日志上的行为、仿真反馈下表现、有限物理 ODD 表现、被报告道路范围内的运行证据。任何一级都不能自动推出量产。[SRC-MAIN-Q7]

评分：2 分为顺序、最窄结论和不可推出项均正确；1 分为顺序正确但缺结论边界；0 分为把离线或 replay 当 fully reactive safety。典型错误：把“真实道路”四字本身当成无限 ODD 的安全证明。

SC-Q7-T 因果追踪

题目：某论文称“在 reactive simulator 的 136 个测试场景上 collision component 提升，因此更安全”。写出还需追踪的五个字段。

答案、评分与典型错误

答案要点：仿真版本/agent reactiveness、136 场景的抽样与 ODD、collision 定义及分母、baseline 与输入/训练是否匹配、失败分布与 fallback；结论应限制在该仿真设置。[SRC-QMAP-Q7]

评分：2 分写出至少五项且限制结论；1 分只列三至四项；0 分直接接受公开道路安全结论。典型错误：只问统计显著性，却不问碰撞口径和场景覆盖。

SC-Q7-X 比较判断

题目：表 A 使用 2 秒 horizon、10 Hz、相机+地图；表 B 使用 4 秒 horizon、2 Hz、仅相机。两表 PDMS 是否可直接横比？

答案、评分与典型错误

答案：不能直接横比。horizon、frequency 和输入条件同时变化；最多作为方向性参考，除非获得统一评测或可验证换算。[SRC-QMAP-Q7]

评分：2 分指出三类不一致及所需统一协议；1 分只说“不可比”但不给原因；0 分按数值直接排名。典型错误：认为指标同名就天然可比。

SC-Q7-D 诊断设计

题目：匿名公司称“模型已量产且比人类安全 5 倍”，只提供一段视频和模型 forward latency。请改写成保守表述并列最小补证。

答案、评分与典型错误

答案示例：“材料显示系统在未完整披露条件的一次道路演示中运行，且模型子模块在指定但未说明的硬件上达到某延迟；尚不足以验证量产规模或相对人类安全率。”补证至少包括 ODD、软件版本、运行规模/里程/时长、干预与事故分母、fallback、端到端 latency、故障监控、独立审计。[SRC-MAIN-Q7]

评分：2 分同时完成降级表述和六项以上补证；1 分只有补证清单或只有保守表述；0 分保留“安全 5 倍”结论。典型错误：把 code/weights 开放等同于部署链可复现。

Q8 如果要复现、评测或继续研究，应如何选择路线和设计最小实验？

本章事实来源：[SRC-MAIN-Q8] = mainline_core.md Q8；[SRC-QMAP-Q8] = q_learning_map.json Q8；[SRC-WC01] = worked_case_trace.json；[SRC-TA] = teaching_audit.json。路线选择建议属于基于上述材料的教学综合，不是系统能力排名。

第 1 步：先给最短答案

先按研究问题选择“需要观察和干预的模块边界”，再按可获得 artifact、预算和评测协议选系统；不要先选榜单最高的模型。最小实验必须冻结 baseline、数据、seed、horizon、controller 和评测环境，只改变一个核心机制，同时记录 action-side 主指标、安全/舒适/时延等软约束以及可复查的下层 artifact，并在运行前写出失败判据。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

Q8 的输出不是“我准备跑代码”，而是一条闭合链：问题 -> 已知/未知 -> 证据包 -> 假设 -> baseline/冻结变量 -> intervention -> 指标/约束 -> artifact -> keep/reject/uncertain/needs-more-evidence。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

第 2 步：明确学习目标和通过标准

本章要求能用统一模板分析未见系统；按决策敏感度排列 unknown；设计 baseline、变量、metrics、constraints 完整的实验；根据证据输出决策；把 WC-01 的方法迁移到未见场景，而不是复制动作序列。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

四类自测共 8 分，6 分达标。若实验同时改变多个核心变量却宣称验证单一机制，或把“代码成功运行”写成“论文主张已复现”，构成关键失败。[SRC-TA]

第 3 步：先区分四种任务

reproduction 询问能否在规定环境下重建论文所报告的 artifact 和指标；evaluation 用既定系统回答新协议下表现；ablation 比较组件开关后的增量；causal validation 要通过匹配控制和干预，排除数据量、算力、参数量、输出接口等替代解释。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

四者可以组合但不能混称。例如成功加载 checkpoint 并跑通十个场景，是运行复现的一部分，不是性能复现；某模块关闭后分数下降，是消融证据，但若同时改变训练数据和 decoder，就不能把差值全部归因于模块。

第 4 步：用统一模板读一个未见系统

十分钟初筛可按八项进行：判定系统边界与 E4/E5 输出；填写输入、表示、temporal、bridge、generator、controller、training、evaluation、artifact；把模块标为 runtime、training-only、external；定位最强 claim 的原文窗口；检查 benchmark 和 baseline 可比性；用 Q6 的 M -> Z -> Y 检查机制；用 Q7 的证据阶梯限制结论；最后写一个单变量实验。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

输出时强制分四栏：reported fact、author claim、bounded inference、unknown。不知道 controller 不是缺点，而是一个需要优先查询的接口断点；只有在它改变当前决策时，才值得投入更多检索或实验成本。

第 5 步：按研究问题选路线，而不是背模型名

若研究 language/action 统一接口，应选择能观察 token/trajectory coupling 的系统；若研究结构化 grounding，应选择显式几何、BEV、agent token 或拓扑路径；若研究 hierarchical bridge，应选择能分离 high-level decision 与 planner 的架构；若研究多模态连续轨迹，应比较 regression、token、diffusion/flow；若研究 world/dynamics，应确认 world branch 是 training-only、runtime condition 还是仅生成辅助；若研究 reasoning/post-training，应要求 action-side outcome，而不是只看语言质量。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

系统名只是定位入口。真正决定实验价值的是：目标模块是否可隔离、代码与权重是否可得、数据和 metric 是否能重建、计算预算是否容许 matched control，以及失败后能否从日志定位原因。

第 6 步：把 unknown 按决策敏感度排序

可以用三问排序：若该字段取不同值，会不会改变系统分类？会不会改变安全或实验可行性？能否以低成本获得？例如 E5 controller 是否外置，可能改变闭环归因，优先级高；未报告的某层 tensor 宽度若不影响当前机制判断，优先级低。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

建议把 unknown 分为 blocker、decision-sensitive、nice-to-have。blocker 未解决就不能运行或判定；decision-sensitive 可先给条件结论；nice-to-have 写入复现日志而不阻塞第一轮。这样既避免把未知硬猜成事实，也避免“必须解决一切才行动”的瘫痪。

第 7 步：写出最小可证伪实验账本

一个完整账本至少含九项：研究问题、可证伪假设、unmodified baseline、唯一 intervention、冻结变量、主指标、soft constraints、下层 artifacts、失败判据。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

冻结变量通常包括数据版本/split、采样和预处理、训练步数、有效 batch、参数规模或 matched compute、seed 集、输出接口、horizon/frequency、controller 和 metric implementation。主指标回答研究问题；soft constraints 防止平均收益掩盖 collision、TTC、comfort、latency、memory 或 failure type 退化。artifact 至少保存配置、checkpoint、prediction、rollout log、视频/可视化、metric code/version 和失败样例。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

可证伪意味着预先允许自己的假设失败。例如“加入 occlusion memory 能改善遮挡场景 E6”必须规定：若只改善 open-loop L2、不改善目标场景闭环结果，或延迟超过预算、非遮挡场景明显退化，则不接受该机制主张。

预注册还应写清分析单位和停止规则。若以场景为单位，就不能在看到结果后改成按帧统计来放大样本量；若计划运行三个 seed，就不能只展示最好的一个。对昂贵闭环评测，可先冻结一个小规模筛选集和一个互不重叠的确认集：筛选集只负责淘汰明显无效方案，确认集负责最终判断。确认集的指标、阈值和失败场景分类应在查看结果前确定。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

第 8 步：最小不等于样本少，而是归因清楚

“只跑一个 seed、十个场景”通常只是便宜，不一定最小。最小实验要保留足以判断的场景切片、重复和统计口径，同时只改变一个核心解释变量。若方差很大，应增加 seeds 或场景数；若运行昂贵，可先用预注册的小规模筛选，再对通过者做确认性运行。

当机制改变输出接口时，归因尤其困难。例如从文本轨迹换成连续 diffusion decoder，同时改变训练目标、推理步数和 controller 接口，差值不能只归于“diffusion”。此时应设计中间对照，或把结论降为 pipeline-level improvement，而非纯组件因果效应。

如果无法构造完全匹配的控制组，不必停止研究，但必须改写问题。与其问“模块 M 是否导致提升”，不如问“包含 M、数据增量和新 decoder 的完整方案在固定预算下是否优于旧 pipeline”。前者要求组件因果证据，后者允许系统级比较。研究问题与可实现控制之间对齐，能避免用一个实验回答它没有能力回答的问题。

第 9 步：建立 artifact 与决策闭环

实验结束后不要只保存最终分数。应先核对配置与实际运行是否一致，再检查 checkpoint、prediction/rollout、指标实现、失败场景和资源记录。随后把结果映射为四种决策：keep 表示在预注册条件下支持且无不可接受退化；reject 表示命中失败判据；uncertain 表示结果方差或协议问题阻止判断；needs-more-evidence 表示 blocker 尚缺但已有方向性信号。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

“运行成功”只说明过程没有表层报错；若 checkpoint 未生成、metric 读取旧目录、评测场景为空或 controller 不是预注册版本，实验仍未完成。下层 artifact 是最终事实源。

故障归因也应进入账本。训练发散、显存不足、评测超时、指标下降和机制无效不是同一种失败：前三者首先否定当前实现或资源配置，不能直接否定科学假设；指标在正确运行后稳定命中失败判据，才构成针对假设的负证据。相反，修复实现后若改变了 batch、数据或优化器，也必须把它视为新实验版本，不能与旧结果无缝拼接。

建议为每次运行分配不可变 run ID，并在结果表中关联配置哈希、代码 revision、数据 manifest、checkpoint 哈希和评测输出路径。这样，任何一个 headline number 都能回到实际 artifact，而不是回到人工记忆。对跨机器或长任务，还应记录中断、恢复和被跳过的场景；否则“完成率”差异可能被误当作模型能力差异。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

第 10 步：纠正两个高频误区

误区一：代码运行等于复现完成。 纠正：复现至少要对齐版本、数据、配置、输出 artifact、指标语义与原 claim。只跑通入口可以标为 environment smoke test。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

误区二：指标提升等于机制验证。 纠正：提升可能来自额外数据、compute、参数、不同 decoder、不同 controller 或选择性报告。需要 matched control、单变量 intervention 和机制侧观测，才能把结论从相关推进到因果。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

第 11 步：冻结 WC-01，并做实验与反事实

到 Q8，WC-01 的 E0 事实、坐标、时间线、ODD 和安全约束全部冻结；它是已见案例，只能作为对照，不得继续改 ground truth。[SRC-WC01]

可以设计四个单变量实验：只替换 officer cue 文本，检查 E4/E6 是否语义一致改变；固定当前帧并移除历史，检查 T2-T4 的 occlusion uncertainty；固定 encoder/reasoning，比较 regression、token、diffusion/flow 的候选质量与时延；逐步降低 cue confidence 或 visibility，检查 fallback 切换是否单调稳定。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

反事实 A：语言因果。 保持图像和动态体不变，把 cue 从“减速后并左绕行”替换为含义冲突且低置信的片段。若轨迹完全不变，language 可能未进入 action generation；若无视视觉安全直接服从文本，也是不合格。

反事实 B：记忆因果。 在 T3 当前帧相同的条件下，一组保留 T0-T2 遮挡历史，一组清除历史。若两组都表现相同，不能据此立刻断言 memory 无效，还要确认模型是否实际读取历史、当前帧是否已足够、评测切片是否有区分度。

新场景 SCN-HO-01 必须至少改变两个因果轴，并重新推导 E2-E6；不能把 WC-01 的“先停、再右转”当通用答案。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

迁移时应保留的是推理程序，而不是动作模板：先列可观测事实，再建立带置信度的 world state，随后按实时约束生成候选并通过 action gate，最后记录 fallback 原因。新场景若把遮挡目标换成后方来车、把施工指挥换成车道控制灯，最佳动作可能完全不同；仍然要求事实与推断分离、导航服从安全约束以及 unknown 不被偷偷补全。这才是从案例中学习到可迁移能力。

第 12 步：四类自测、答案与评分

SC-Q8-C 概念辨析

题目：分别定义 reproduction、evaluation、ablation、causal validation，并指出“关闭模块后分数下降”属于哪一类证据。

答案、评分与典型错误

答案：四类定义见第 3 步；关闭模块是 ablation，只有在数据、compute、接口等匹配且干预对应机制时，才可能升级为较强 causal evidence。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

评分：2 分定义和边界完整；1 分能区分但把 ablation 自动当因果；0 分把运行成功当全部任务。典型错误：认为 reproduction 必须逐 bit 相同，或反过来认为任意近似运行都算复现。

SC-Q8-T 流程追踪

题目：把“历史记忆是否改善遮挡决策”写成 evidence -> experiment -> artifact -> decision 流程。

答案、评分与典型错误

答案要点：先确认模型历史输入和目标场景证据；以完整模型为 baseline，仅移除/打乱历史且冻结其余变量；记录目标切片 closed-loop、安全、延迟及不确定性；保存配置、checkpoint、rollout 和失败样例；按预注册阈值输出 keep/reject/uncertain。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

评分：2 分链条闭合且有失败判据；1 分有实验但无 artifact/decision；0 分同时换模型和数据。典型错误：只看总体平均分，不看遮挡切片。

SC-Q8-X 比较判断

题目：方案 A 固定全部条件，只 shuffle reasoning text；方案 B 同时加入 reasoning 数据、扩大模型并更换 decoder。哪一个更适合验证 reasoning 因果贡献？

答案、评分与典型错误

答案：A 的可归因性更强；B 适合比较整套 pipeline，但不能把提升单独归因于 reasoning。B 可能工程结果更好，却结论强度更弱。[SRC-QMAP-Q8]

评分：2 分同时讨论归因、成本和结论范围；1 分只选 A；0 分因 B 分数可能更高就认为其因果证据更强。典型错误：把“系统最优”与“机制已验证”混为一谈。

SC-Q8-D 实验设计

题目：为“多候选 generator 改善安全候选覆盖”提交最小实验。

答案、评分与典型错误

答案示例：baseline 为单候选 regression；intervention 仅替换为匹配参数/训练预算的 K 候选 generator，encoder、数据、horizon、controller 和训练步数冻结；主指标为目标场景至少一个无碰撞可行候选的覆盖率及最终 closed-loop 结果；约束为 latency、memory、comfort；artifact 为候选集、打分、最终选择和 rollout；失败判据为覆盖率无提升、最终选择不改善、或延迟超过预算。[SRC-MAIN-Q8]

评分：2 分包含 baseline、唯一变量、冻结项、指标、约束、artifact、失败判据；1 分缺两项以内；0 分同时换数据、模型规模和 controller。典型错误：只报告 oracle best-of-K，却不报告实际 selector 的结果。

结业迁移：未见系统与未见场景

本结业任务在 Q1-Q8 之后开放。系统材料来自隔离的 SYS-HO-01 learner packet；场景 SCN-HO-01 是此前正文未出现的合成场景。请独立完成全部提交；标准答案与评分记录由隔离的 evaluator 持有，不进入 learner-facing HTML，也不在本章公开定位信息。[SRC-HO-PACKET][SRC-HO-MANIFEST]

A. 必要原始片段

以下片段保留论文原文，只做长度裁剪。它们是作答材料，不是提示答案。

片段 P1：输入。 “The visual input ... comprises six surround-view images. The instruction ... provides navigation commands ... The ego state ... encapsulates the current velocity, acceleration, and a 2-second historical trajectory ... sampled at 2 Hz.” [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L146]

片段 P2：融合与分支。 “The vision, text, and agent tokens are concatenated ... and fed into the LLM ... O_agent ... is routed to a specialized world head for current 3D perception and future trajectory prediction ... O_vision and O_text ... generate textual responses.” [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L149-L162]

片段 P3：轨迹生成。 “Let x_t in R^(N_m x H x 3) denote the joint multi-agent action noises ... historical ego actions ... and ... VLM output tokens ... The generation decoder ... estimate[s] the denoised multi-agent trajectories.” [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L173-L203]

片段 P4：训练。 Stage 2 combines language modeling and world-cognition losses; Stage 3 freezes the VLM and trains ADDT with diffusion denoising plus representation alignment; Stage 4 combines diffusion policy optimization with behavior-cloning regularization and a reward using ego PDMS and surrounding-agent trajectory error. [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L228-L270]

片段 P5：协议与结果。 The paper evaluates on NAVSIM v1/v2; NAVSIM data are partitioned into 1,192 training and 136 test scenes. The reported NAVSIM v1 row after four stages is NC 99.4, DAC 98.8, TTC 98.5, comfort 100, ego progress 87.1, PDMS 92.9. [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L272-L320]

片段 P6：消融。 The accumulated stage table reports PDMS 84.4, 85.5, 89.3 and 92.9; its caption states that IDs 1-2 generate textual trajectory tokens while IDs 3-4 use continuous ADDT actions. The ADDT table reports five-step ADDT 89.3 at 0.106 s and twenty-step ADDT 89.6 at 0.383 s. [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L359-L379][SYS-HO-01-TEX:L417-L447]

片段 P7：限制与材料完整性。 The conclusion states that semantic cognition focuses on agents and omits future road-geometry and map-topology evolution. The downloaded packet contains complete HTML, main TeX and a 20-page PDF, but the repeatedly referenced Supplemental Material is absent; no repository/project-page URL or low-level controller interface was found in the packet. [SYS-HO-01-TEX:L539-L544][SRC-HO-MANIFEST]

B. 新场景 SCN-HO-01

以下均为合成教学事实，不是法规或产品规格。[SCN-HO-01-CONTRACT]

  • 右侧通行城市快速路，夜间中雨，路面湿滑；ego 在三车道同向路段以 16 m/s 行驶。
  • 导航缓存仍要求 180 m 后从右侧出口驶出，但该指令最后更新时间为 45 秒前。
  • 前相机因雨滴和眩光退化，车道灯识别置信度为 0.58；左/右侧相机可用，后相机可见一辆开启警灯和警报器的应急车辆快速接近。
  • 车道上方控制灯：当前车道为绿色向下箭头；右侧出口车道显示红色 X，但由于前相机退化，只能由侧视几何和连续三帧观测间接确认；左侧相邻车道在最近三帧保持绿色箭头。
  • 地图只提供静态车道拓扑，不包含临时车道控制；无 V2X、无远程驾驶员、无隐藏 ground truth。
  • T0：应急车辆距 ego 后方约 70 m。T1=2 s：约 42 m。T2=4 s：约 24 m；右侧出口分流点距 ego 约 85 m。
  • 教学约束：不得进入确认或疑似关闭/反向车道；湿滑路面正常减速度绝对值不超过 2.0 m/s²；不得无后向风险分析地紧急制动；允许错过出口；若车道控制语义或让行空间无法可靠确认，选择可控 minimum-risk fallback。

C. 作答任务

独立提交以下内容，再由隔离的 evaluator key 按 rubric 评分：

  1. 给 SYS-HO-01 做 VLA 边界分类，给置信度、决定性接口证据和 unknown。
  2. 用 E0-E6 追踪系统，逐项标 reported / inferred / unknown。
  3. 重建训练阶段，把每项能力映射到数据、target/loss/reward 和冻结组件。
  4. 将 P1-P7 中内容分为作者主张、直接实验事实、你的有界推断和不支持的结论。
  5. 审计主结果和至少两个消融：它们证明什么、没有证明什么。
  6. 列出显式限制、缺失 artifact 和至少三个额外证据边界。
  7. 为一个机制主张设计最小可证伪实验：baseline、唯一 intervention、冻结变量、主指标、soft constraints、artifact、失败判据。
  8. 对 SCN-HO-01 写 E0-E6、事实/推断/未知、T0-T2 行为门、fallback 和一个反事实。不得复制 WC-01 的动作序列。

D. 五维评分 rubric

维度分值满分表现
识别与边界10依据输入、action coupling 和训练目标分类，明确 controller 等 unknown
机制解释20E0-E6 连续，模块、训练信号和 runtime/training-only/external 边界清楚
应用与场景迁移25对新场景重新推导事实、推断、约束、时序和 fallback，不照抄 WC-01
证据批判20区分事实/作者主张/推断/未知，审计可比性、替代解释和 claim ceiling
实验设计25baseline、单变量、冻结项、指标、约束、artifact 和失败判据完整

每个系统/场景任务至少 70 分，综合平均至少 75；证据批判至少 12/20，实验设计至少 15/25，且无 critical failure。

Critical failure 包括：捏造来源事实；把 unknown 写成确定；把 open-loop/planning benchmark 当公开道路安全证明；忽略明确碰撞/车道约束；在不确定时强行进入冲突区；同时改变多个核心变量却宣称验证单一机制。

架构矩阵：25 个核心系统与 1 个边界锚点

矩阵按 canonical family 去重。18 个字段中的“未报告”表示证据缺口，不表示模块不存在；Core 身份只代表越过 action-output gate。

阅读时先比较输入、表示、reasoning-to-action bridge、action representation、decoder/controller boundary，再比较 training 与 evaluation；这样可以避免把名称相似但接口不同的系统错误归为同一路线。

8 个共通 Runtime 模块

RM-01 至 RM-08 是跨路线反复出现的可归一模块：感知、空间 grounding、视觉语言核心、时间记忆、world/dynamics、action bridge、tokenizer/codebook 和 trajectory/action decoder。Common 不表示每个系统都显式实现全部八项。

这组骨架的作用是让读者沿同一接口链比较不同论文，而不是把 Transformer、CoT、MoE 或 diffusion 当成互斥的系统类别；每个模块仍要检查输入输出、坐标、频率、runtime 与 training-only 边界。

RM-01Visual / multimodal perception encoder

camera images and, where applicable, LiDAR/ego/navigation context → visual, BEV, query or multimodal tokens

类别
common
覆盖系统
26
边界说明
Sensor modality and camera count are configuration, not separate modules. BEV/query/geometry branches are split into RM-02 only when they perform explicit scene grounding.
RM-02Geometry / scene grounding module

visual features plus optional depth, LiDAR-supervised priors or BEV features → spatially grounded BEV, object, map, occupancy, pointmap or geometry tokens

类别
common
覆盖系统
10
边界说明
LiDAR pointmap supervision in VLGA and frozen VGGT/Cosmos teachers in LaST-VLA are training mechanisms; the retained runtime modules are the camera-derived geometry expert or latent geometry stream.
RM-03Vision-language reasoning core

visual/grounded tokens, language/navigation prompt and ego context → semantic hidden states, reasoning tokens, intent or action-conditioned context

类别
common
覆盖系统
26
边界说明
CoT, latent CoT and structured reasoning are representations or execution regimes inside the core unless a separately identifiable bridge/latent module computes them. SFT/GRPO are never part of this runtime module.
RM-04Temporal context encoder / memory

past frames, past ego states/actions or prior hidden states → bounded temporal context tokens or memory queries

类别
common
覆盖系统
8
边界说明
Merely concatenating multiple frames or listing a history horizon is not enough. A system is included only when the packet identifies temporal compression, memory, trajectory encoding or recurrent context processing.
RM-05World / dynamics prediction module

current scene/context and optional candidate/historical action → future frame/BEV/dynamics tokens or predictive latent conditions consumed by planning

类别
common
覆盖系统
6
边界说明
DriveVLA-W0 is excluded from the runtime list because its packet explicitly says image generation is bypassed during driving inference; its world-model component belongs in a pretraining-only component table. Future-video FVD is evaluation, not this module.
RM-06Reasoning-to-action bridge

reasoning hidden states, language intent, meta-action or predictive latent → planner conditioning tokens, embeddings, anchors or query states

类别
common
覆盖系统
13
边界说明
This module ends at planner conditioning. It is not the trajectory decoder itself. Plain language output or CoT without an identified conditioning mechanism remains inside RM-03.
RM-07Action / trajectory tokenizer or codebook

continuous trajectory/action or planner hidden state → discrete action tokens, code indices or anchor IDs and their inverse mapping

类别
common
覆盖系统
8
边界说明
A discrete representation is not automatically a tokenizer. Inclusion requires a codebook, tokenization/detokenization operation or anchor lookup. Continuous waypoint regression belongs only to RM-08.
RM-08Trajectory / action decoder

reasoning/action conditions, queries, tokens, anchors or predictive latents → waypoints, continuous trajectory, action tokens or high-level driving action

类别
common
覆盖系统
25
边界说明
The decoder ends at planned action/trajectory. PID or actuator conversion is RM-09. AlphaDrive is included only for its high-level meta-action language decoder; its downstream continuous action model is outside AlphaDrive and must not be attributed to it. DynVLA is omitted because the packet marks planner_decoder unreported.

8 个路线/系统特有 Runtime 模块

RM-09、RM-10、RM-13、RM-14 是 route-unique，RM-11、RM-12、RM-15、RM-16 是 single-system-unique。它们分别刻画 controller、expert partition、candidate selection、reflection、dynamic routing、cross-modal causal attention 与 action refinement。

特有模块是判断真实创新位置的关键：同样写 MoE，可能路由 token、参数或 specialist branch；同样写 planning，可能只是候选生成，也可能包含 scorer、selector 与显式 refinement。训练方法和数据集不应混入此表。

RM-09Low-level controller

planned waypoints or trajectory → steering, throttle and brake commands

类别
route_unique
覆盖系统
3
边界说明
Do not label every trajectory planner as a controller. A controller requires an explicit waypoint/trajectory-to-actuation conversion. Unreported controller cells remain unknown, not absent.
RM-10Multi-expert partition / specialist topology

modality- or task-specific token streams → specialist hidden states joined by attention, fusion or shared context

类别
route_unique
覆盖系统
5
边界说明
Fixed specialist branches or MoT partitions are topology, not routing. Dynamic route selection/weighting is separately RM-11.
RM-11Scene-adaptive expert router

BEV scene representation → per-sample expert weights and a merged FFN parameterization

类别
single_system_unique
覆盖系统
1
边界说明
This is sample/scene-level parameter fusion, not token-level top-k dispatch. UniDriveVLA and VLGA have expert partitions but no evidence of dynamic expert routing in their packet cells.
RM-12Ego-adaptive token router

visual tokens conditioned on ego state → selected/routed critical visual tokens

类别
single_system_unique
覆盖系统
1
边界说明
Token selection is not expert routing. Regex rules must not collapse the word router into RM-11.
RM-13Candidate generator / scorer / selector

multiple trajectory/action candidates and confidence/context features → ranked or selected trajectory/action candidate

类别
route_unique
覆盖系统
5
边界说明
A multimodal decoder is not automatically a selector. ORION is included at medium confidence because its packet states a generative multimodal planner but gives less explicit selection mechanics than AnchorVLA/ColaVLA/HiST-VLA.
RM-14Explicit reflection loop

initial reasoning/trajectory or self-generated future image → revised reasoning or corrected action/trajectory

类别
route_unique
覆盖系统
2
边界说明
Generic CoT or a reflection field in a training corpus is insufficient. The packet must state that inference performs a check/revision or reasons over a self-generated future.
RM-15Conditional cross-modal causal attention

BEV, world, language, ego-state and action tokens → causally conditioned action-token hidden states

类别
single_system_unique
覆盖系统
1
边界说明
CMCA is an inference-time attention mechanism. Training-only causal masks used to prevent shortcut learning, such as LaST-VLA's structured causal anti-shortcut mask, must not be merged here without runtime evidence.
RM-16Action Refinement Module

parallel continuous action-query predictions → refined continuous trajectory chunk

类别
single_system_unique
覆盖系统
1
边界说明
ARM is downstream of the VL-to-Action cross-attention decoder and should not be used as an alias for the entire planner.

版本与接口演化：2022-2026

演化不是单一榜单上的线性替代，而是 action interface、representation bottleneck 与 evaluation protocol 三条轨同时展开。Paper、repository、checkpoint、product 和 deployment 日期在 canonical timeline 中分别记录。

早期系统建立 language-guided waypoint 与 hierarchical decision；随后出现 3D grounding、action token、planning token、diffusion planner 和 reasoning post-training；2026 年重点进一步转向 world/dynamics、latent reasoning、expert topology、geometry specialization 与 streaming memory。

争议与未决问题

以下冲突来自同一来源内部、paper/repo 表述差异或证据层级不匹配。Resolution 是本报告采用的保守裁决，不抹去原始冲突。

AutoDrive-R2 · NAVSIM result

A：Table 2 lists AutoDrive-R2 7B as NC 98.5, DAC 95.9, TTC 95.4, Comfort 100, EP 82.7, PDMS 89.1.

B：The adjacent prose claims NC 98.3, TTC 95.6, DAC 94.4, EP 81.6, and PDMS 90.3.

裁决：Treat the NAVSIM result as source-conflicted; retain table value 89.1 and do not assert the prose value as settled.

AutoDrive-R2 · training duration

A：Section 3.1 reports 750 training iterations and 1 epoch for 18 hours.

B：Appendix A.1 states the training process ran for 750 epochs.

裁决：Use the detailed main-text configuration as primary and flag Appendix A.1's 750 epochs as likely erroneous.

AutoDrive-R2 · dataset authorship and validation

A：Section 2.1 says Qwen2.5-VL-72B generated chains and Qwen-VL-Max validated them.

B：The Ethics and Reproducibility statements describe 6,000 manually annotated image-trajectory pairs.

裁决：The exact amount and role of human annotation are unresolved; describe the corpus as model-generated and expert-model-filtered with claimed manual annotation not operationally specified.

AutoDrive-R2 · physics-grounded reward semantics

A：The main equations penalize MSE to ground-truth position, steering, and velocity plus consecutive-control variation.

B：The prose repeatedly describes these terms as enforcing strict real-world physical feasibility; Appendix C gives hard kinematic quantities that are not directly included in Eq.8.

裁决：Characterize the reward as physics-motivated soft supervision, not a formal hard feasibility guarantee.

AutoVLA · artifact release

A：Project page promises code, weights, and datasets.

B：Repository states reasoning data is pending approval.

裁决：Code/checkpoints are supported; reasoning corpus completeness is not.

ColaVLA · NeuroNCAP headline metrics

A：Table 2 reports score 3.48 and average collision rate 36.8%, with static collision 32.3%.

B：Section 4.2 prose reports score 3.16, average collision rate 42.5%, and static collision 15.0%.

裁决：Retain both evidence windows and mark the closed-loop headline as source-conflicted.

CoWorld-VLA · closed-loop metric label

A：Table 1 calls NAVSIM scores closed-loop metrics.

B：Section 4.1 defines NAVSIM v1 as a non-reactive open-loop protocol.

裁决：Describe NAVSIM as planning-oriented non-reactive simulation while retaining the table's metric terminology only in quotations.

DriveVLA-W0 · causal interpretation of scaling

A：Main text attributes transfer/scaling gains to world modeling.

B：Appendix B.2 labels the FID-PDMS relationship as positive correlation; the largest evidence uses private data.

裁决：Accept within-experiment mechanism evidence, but do not generalize to a universal causal scaling law.

DriveVLM · reproducibility

A：Detailed performance and deployment results are reported.

B：Training data are proprietary or mined.

裁决：Treat results as paper evidence but not fully reproducible evidence.

DriveWorld-VLA · closed-loop terminology

A：NAVSIM tables are described as closed-loop planning.

B：The dataset description calls NAVSIM v1 a non-reactive open-loop simulation protocol and NAVSIM v2 pseudo-simulation.

裁决：Report benchmark-native scores, while labeling the simulator interaction limits explicitly.

DynVLA · interpretability versus reliability

A：Dynamics tokens are visualized as transferable and interpretable.

B：Appendix F shows ambiguous or wrong inferred dynamics in difficult inputs.

裁决：Treat interpretability as inspectability of a compact latent, not guaranteed correctness.

OpenDriveVLA · release completeness

A：Paper presents the full staged training pipeline.

B：Repository snapshot still has release TODOs for training scripts/checkpoints.

裁决：Treat architecture as paper-confirmed and artifact completeness as partial.

ORION · benchmark generalization

A：ORION is strongly competitive on Bench2Drive closed-loop evaluation.

B：Appendix C reports suboptimal nuScenes open-loop results relative to other VLM methods.

裁决：Treat the evidence as benchmark-dependent: strong interactive Bench2Drive performance does not imply uniform open-loop transfer superiority.

Reasoning-VLA · SFT learning rate

A：Section 5.1 states SFT starts at 5e-4.

B：Appendix B.3.1 lists learning_rate 5e-5.

裁决：Treat the exact SFT learning rate as unresolved; RL LR 1e-6 is consistent.

ReCogDrive · reasoning contribution

A：The framework is presented as cognition-guided and the VLM can emit high-level instructions or CoT.

B：The guidance-mode ablation reports no gain from high-level commands and a 0.1 PDMS decrease from CoT on NAVSIM.

裁决：Attribute the strongest NAVSIM gain to learned VLM latent priors, diffusion decoding, and DiffGRPO, not to explicit test-time CoT.

ReCogDrive · Bench2Drive driving score

A：Table 2 displays ReCogDrive DS as 71.36.

B：The prose states Driving Score 71.36, while the surrounding claim calls all results top-performing despite multi-ability mean 42.03 not being the highest row in every category.

裁决：Preserve the reported DS/SR numbers but avoid a blanket claim of dominance across every Bench2Drive sub-ability.

UniDriveVLA · state-of-the-art wording

A：Project/paper use broad state-of-the-art language.

B：The same tables show lower success and mean multi-ability than ORION, and higher PDM-Lite systems are footnoted separately.

裁决：Use metric-specific claims: best DS/efficiency among non-PDM-Lite rows, not universal dominance.

Uni-World VLA · closed-loop wording

A：The paper describes interleaving as a closed-loop interaction and reports NAVSIM closed-loop planning metrics.

B：Inference feeds back self-generated visual tokens, not fresh observations from a reactive environment.

裁决：Call it an internal autoregressive prediction-planning loop; retain NAVSIM protocol label separately.

VLAWorld · generation quality versus safety

A：VLA-World reports best FID among listed autoregressive generators.

B：The paper itself states pixel fidelity is weakly coupled to driving safety.

裁决：Use FID only for generation quality; use planning metrics and reward ablations for planning claims.

五个完整机制解剖

OpenDriveVLA

技术路线：3d-grounded-vla · hierarchical-alignment · autoregressive-trajectory · open-loop

动作边界：tokenized continuous-trajectory waypoints

解码器：autoregressive textual waypoint tokens -> numeric coordinate decoder

控制器：not reported; packet stops at trajectory waypoints

评测范围：open_loop / auxiliary

AutoVLA

技术路线：adaptive-reasoning · physical-action-token · SFT · GRPO-RFT · open-and-closed-loop

动作边界：2048-token physical vocabulary decoded to continuous trajectory

解码器：codebook lookup from action tokens to trajectory; Best-of-N is an optional inference policy

控制器：PID converts trajectory to steering/throttle/brake in CARLA

评测范围：open_loop / nonreactive_pseudo_sim / reactive_closed_loop / auxiliary

DriveVLA-W0

技术路线：world-model · data-scaling · dense-supervision · MoE-action-expert

动作边界：FAST action tokens or continuous trajectories

解码器：500M joint-attention MoE action expert with query, autoregressive, or flow-matching decoder variants

控制器：not reported

评测范围：nonreactive_pseudo_sim / auxiliary

UniDriveVLA

技术路线：mixture-of-transformers · expert-decoupling · sparse-perception · flow-matching · multi-task

动作边界：flow-matching continuous future trajectory

解码器：dedicated action Transformer expert with flow matching

控制器：not reported

评测范围：open_loop / reactive_closed_loop / auxiliary

ORION

技术路线：closed-loop · qt-former · planning-token · generative-planner · three-stage-training

动作边界：multimodal continuous future trajectories

解码器：planning-token-conditioned generative trajectory planner

控制器：not reported; evaluation consumes planned trajectories in Bench2Drive

评测范围：open_loop / reactive_closed_loop / auxiliary

分层学习路径

学习顺序应由读者已有背景决定，但每条路线最终都要回到 action gate、E0-E6 和 evidence ladder。暂时忽略不相关的榜单、模型规模和宣传标签，先产出一个可检查的系统边界、模块接口或实验 artifact，再逐步增加训练与评测细节。

下面六条路径都以“读什么、暂时忽略什么、验证什么、产出什么”为准绳，目标不是背诵系统名，而是能把后续新方案放入同一 18 维矩阵，识别 runtime、training-only 与 external component，并写出包含失败判据的最小可证伪实验。

零自动驾驶背景

先读 Q1-Q3 与 E0-E6；暂时忽略模型年份和榜单；产出一张系统边界图。

CV / 多模态背景

重点读 grounding、temporal、world representation；产出 RM-01..RM-06 接口表。

LLM / VLM 背景

重点读 action bridge、tokenizer、reasoning/post-training；产出 language intervention。

规划控制背景

重点读 E4-E6、controller boundary、closed-loop evidence；产出统一 horizon 对比。

复现导向

先选 artifact 完整系统，冻结 baseline/seed/split/controller；产出可重复 rollout。

研究导向

从 controversy 和 unknown 出发，设计单变量、可证伪、跨证据层实验。

术语表

术语入门解释工程误区
VLA视觉与语言条件直接参与动作生成的模型。必须检查 action gate，不能把任意 Driving VLM 都算作 policy。
Action GateE3 语义/目标到 E4 可执行候选之间的边界。高层 meta-action 若仍依赖外部 action model，不算完整 policy。
BEV鸟瞰坐标中的空间表示。BEV canvas 不等于可靠检测范围。
Planning token从 VLM hidden state 读取规划条件的紧凑 token。可解释名称不保证真实因果语义。
Action token离散化后的 waypoint/trajectory/action 单元。统一自回归接口会引入量化与序列时延。
Diffusion / Flow学习条件连续轨迹分布的生成器。多样性必须对应可行候选，而非随机差异。
World model预测未来或学习 dynamics representation 的模块。视觉生成质量不等于 planning utility。
Visible CoT显式输出的推理文本。可读不等于忠实，也可能增加时延。
Latent CoT隐藏状态中的推理过程。更紧凑但更难审计。
GRPO / RFT基于组内相对奖励的 post-training。reward 设计不同，不能只按算法名横比。
Open-loop环境不响应模型动作的离线评测。不能直接证明 closed-loop safety。
Non-reactive沿固定日志或预录世界评测。其他交通参与者不响应 ego。
Pseudo-simulation近似环境响应的仿真。不等同 fully reactive simulator。
Fallback不确定或风险超阈值时的最小风险策略。停车可以是成功结果，不应只奖励导航完成。
Controller boundarytrajectory 到 steering/throttle/brake 的接口。仿真能运行不等于论文报告了 controller。
Artifact strengthpaper、code、weights、data、eval scripts 的完整度。“开源”不是二元标签。

研究机会

已经明确
id
EST-01
标题
严格 driving-policy VLA 的动作门可以被操作化
summary
视觉、语言与车辆状态必须在同一学习系统边界内直接参与 waypoint、trajectory、action token 或连续动作的生成。只输出高层 meta-action、再交给系统外 action model 的方案属于边界案例；能解释场景也不等于语言已因果参与动作。
claim_ids
BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C1 · BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C9 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C1 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C2 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-C1 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-C3
evidence_ids
BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E1 · BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E5 · BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E9 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E1 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E2 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E1 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E3
controversy_ids
module_ids
RM-02 · RM-03 · RM-06 · RM-07 · RM-08
claim_ceiling
可判定论文所述系统是否跨过 E4 动作门；不能仅凭结构连接断言语言被实际使用、解释忠实或系统可部署。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
对同一视觉序列执行语言删除、语义相反替换、样本间 shuffle 与无关文本替换，固定模型、随机种子和 decoder，比较 E4 轨迹方向、约束违例与不确定性变化。
failure_criteria
四种语言干预对 E4 的影响不超过重复运行噪声，或只改变文本解释而不改变动作；此时应把结论降为“结构上接入语言或辅助解释”。
odd_benchmark_boundary
适用于有明确 E4 输出定义的离线数据、open-loop 或仿真；不能由分类本身外推到真实道路安全。
已经明确
id
EST-02
标题
VLA 的演化是接口重排，不是模块消失
summary
现有系统仍反复出现感知、几何 grounding、语言推理、时序记忆、reasoning-to-action bridge、动作表示、decoder、selector 与 controller。所谓端到端主要改变模块的训练耦合与信息接口，而不是取消几何、planner 或执行层。
claim_ids
BATCH-A-UniDriveVLA::UV-C1 · BATCH-A-UniDriveVLA::UV-C3 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-C3 · BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-C4 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C6
evidence_ids
BATCH-A-UniDriveVLA::UV-E1 · BATCH-A-UniDriveVLA::UV-E3 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-E3 · BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-E4 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E6
controversy_ids
module_ids
RM-01 · RM-02 · RM-03 · RM-04 · RM-06 · RM-08 · RM-09 · RM-10
claim_ceiling
可支持一套跨系统的运行时接口 taxonomy；不能据此断言某种 topology 普遍优于模块化栈。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
为同一系统记录 E0-E6 的 shape、坐标系、时间戳、频率和 ownership，执行 frame 变换、时间平移、controller swap 与接口断连测试。
failure_criteria
若系统无法在不依赖隐含后处理的情况下重建 E0-E6，或 controller swap 造成的 E6 变化大于被宣称的 planner 增益，则“纯模型端到端收益”被否证。
odd_benchmark_boundary
接口结论跨 benchmark 可复用；性能结论仍受传感器、horizon、controller、场景和指标实现限制。
已经明确
id
EST-03
标题
动作生成已形成多条可辨识路线
summary
离散 waypoint/action token、并行连续 action query、diffusion/flow matching、anchor-residual 与候选评分是不同的 E4 生成和选择机制。路线标签只有落到 RM-06、RM-07、RM-08 和 RM-13 的接口后才具有比较意义。
claim_ids
BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C2 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-C3 · BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-C2 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-C1 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-C4 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-C3
evidence_ids
BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E2 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E3 · BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-E2 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E1 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E4 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E3
controversy_ids
BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::CONFLICT-15
module_ids
RM-06 · RM-07 · RM-08 · RM-13 · RM-16
claim_ceiling
可描述各路线在量化、并行性、多模态候选和选择机制上的设计差异；不能用不同协议的 headline score 排出普适优劣。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
固定 encoder、训练数据、horizon、控制器、采样预算和总推理 FLOPs，比较 regression、AR 与 diffusion/flow，并分别报告 oracle best-of-K、实际 selector、单样本 E4 和 reactive E6。
failure_criteria
若 matched-compute 下路线差异消失，或 oracle best-of-K 提升无法被 selector 转化为 E6 提升，则“生成器天然更强”的主张失败。
odd_benchmark_boundary
至少同时覆盖多模态交互切片与普通直行切片；NAVSIM、nuScenes、Bench2Drive 结果不得无协议换算直接合并。
已经明确
id
EST-04
标题
证据层级决定结论上限
summary
离线轨迹误差、open-loop score、non-reactive replay、pseudo-simulation、reactive simulator、封闭场地和真实道路回答的是不同问题。环境是否响应 ego 行为，是闭环主张能否成立的关键分界。
claim_ids
BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-C5 · BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-C5 · BATCH-B-SimLingo::SL-C4 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-C5 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-C8
evidence_ids
BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-E5 · BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-E5 · BATCH-B-SimLingo::SL-E4 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E5 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E8
controversy_ids
BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::CONFLICT-10 · BATCH-A-ORION::CONFLICT-13
module_ids
RM-08 · RM-09
claim_ceiling
可在各自协议内报告最窄能力结论；没有 reactive feedback、失败分布和 ODD 报告时，不能声称真实交通闭环安全。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
把同一模型依次放入 log replay、非反应仿真和 reactive simulator，保持场景种子、controller 与指标口径一致，测量排名、碰撞类型和 progress 的变化。
failure_criteria
若模型排名或安全结论随 reactiveness 改变，任何由低层协议外推到高层闭环的结论均失败。
odd_benchmark_boundary
每项结果必须绑定 simulator/version、agent policy、场景抽样、碰撞分母、sensor、horizon 和 controller。
仍不确定
id
UNC-01
标题
显式 CoT 是否忠实地中介动作仍不确定
summary
AutoVLA 支持自适应 reasoning 与效率联合优化，但 ReCogDrive 中加入显式 CoT 并未提升 NAVSIM PDMS。现有结果不能区分可见文本是动作中介、训练正则、事后解释，还是与 latent policy 并行的副产物。
claim_ids
BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C1 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C3 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C4 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-C7
evidence_ids
BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E1 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E3 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E4 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E7
controversy_ids
BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::CONFLICT-15
module_ids
RM-03 · RM-06 · RM-14
claim_ceiling
只能说某些系统训练或输出 reasoning，且在特定设置与性能相关；不能说可见 CoT 忠实解释或因果控制动作。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
对 matched scene 构造正确、错误、反事实、shuffle 和隐藏 CoT，测 E4/E6 的定向变化，并用隐藏状态 probe 检查动作改变是否经由预注册中介。
failure_criteria
错误 CoT 不按语义方向改变动作、动作先于 CoT 确定，或只有文本指标变化而 E4/E6 不变。
odd_benchmark_boundary
必须包含需要语义区分的交互场景；纯直行或可由数据先验猜测的 QA 不能验证 faithfulness。
仍不确定
id
UNC-02
标题
world representation 到 planning utility 的因果链尚未闭合
summary
future-image、BEV imagination、dynamics latent 和教师特征可能提供有用监督，但图像更逼真或 future loss 更低不等于 policy 使用了该表示。现有证据多为同论文 ablation 或相关性。
claim_ids
BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-C2 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-C5 · BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-C3 · BATCH-D-VLAWorld::VW-C3 · BATCH-E-LaST-VLA::LST-C2
evidence_ids
BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-E2 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-E5 · BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-E3 · BATCH-D-VLAWorld::VW-E3 · BATCH-E-LaST-VLA::LST-E2 · BATCH-E-LaST-VLA::LST-E3
controversy_ids
BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::CONFLICT-8 · BATCH-D-VLAWorld::CONFLICT-19
module_ids
RM-04 · RM-05 · RM-06
claim_ceiling
可说 world/dynamics 目标改善了特定论文设置中的表示或规划指标；不能建立普适的生成质量、因果使用与安全收益链。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
固定参数和 compute，用同容量 temporal block 替代 world module，并进行正确 latent、错误 latent、时间错位 latent 和零 latent 注入，观察 E4 与 reactive E6。
failure_criteria
world metric 显著变化但 E4/E6 不变，或 temporal control 达到同等收益；此时撤回“world model 为规划提供独立因果贡献”。
odd_benchmark_boundary
需覆盖遮挡、动态交互与反事实未来；只在 open-loop 平均 L2 或 FID 上成立的结论不得外推。
仍不确定
id
UNC-03
标题
跨系统实时性比较缺乏统一测量合同
summary
多篇工作把大 VLM 延迟列为部署约束，也有系统报告较低单次轨迹生成时间；但硬件、precision、batch、相机数、语言路径、采样步数、controller rate 和预后处理通常不同。
claim_ids
BATCH-B-DriveVLM::DV-C6 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-C6 · BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-C10 · BATCH-E-VLGA::VLG-C7
evidence_ids
BATCH-B-DriveVLM::DV-E6 · BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E6 · BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-E10 · BATCH-E-VLGA::VLG-E12
controversy_ids
module_ids
RM-01 · RM-03 · RM-08 · RM-09 · RM-13
claim_ceiling
只能报告各论文给定硬件与软件栈下的局部时延；不能据此制作跨系统实时性排名。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
在同一 GPU、precision、batch=1、固定 view count、相同 horizon 和 warm-up 下，分解 perception、reasoning、generator、selector、controller 的 p50/p95/p99 延迟与显存。
failure_criteria
若 headline latency 无法复现，或完整端到端 p99 超过 ODD 控制周期而仅 forward p50 达标，则实时主张失败。
odd_benchmark_boundary
至少分别报告离线吞吐、单车在线推理和闭环控制周期；服务器 H200 结果不能直接代表车端 Orin 级部署。
仍不确定
id
UNC-04
标题
真实道路安全、fallback 与故障恢复证据普遍不足
summary
现有代表系统多数停留在 open-loop 或仿真，少量闭环结果也常缺少统一的 failure denominator、干预定义、minimum-risk maneuver、软件版本和独立审计。平均分不能回答严重尾部风险。
claim_ids
BATCH-C-AutoDrive-R2::ADR2-C7 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-C6 · BATCH-E-HiST-VLA::HST-C9 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-C6
evidence_ids
BATCH-C-AutoDrive-R2::ADR2-E7 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E6 · BATCH-E-HiST-VLA::HST-E9 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E6
controversy_ids
BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::CONFLICT-16
module_ids
RM-08 · RM-09 · RM-13
claim_ceiling
可以确认研究原型在有限数据或仿真中的能力与已报告限制；不能断言公开道路安全、量产成熟度或故障可控。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
在预注册 ODD 内构建遮挡、施工、传感退化、旧导航和多车博弈切片，报告每类暴露次数、碰撞/near-miss、干预、minimum-risk stop、恢复时间和置信校准。
failure_criteria
严重失败率置信上界超过预注册阈值，fallback 在高不确定场景仍追求 progress，或结果无法按场景和软件版本回溯。
odd_benchmark_boundary
仿真结论仅限对应 simulator、agent policy 与场景库；真实道路结论必须注明地理、天气、速度、道路类型和运营约束。
被过度宣传
id
HYP-01
标题
把 non-reactive 或 pseudo-simulation 宣传为完整闭环
summary
环境不响应 ego 行为时，评测不能证明交互稳定性。NAVSIM v1 的 non-reactive 与 v2 的 pseudo-simulation 有价值，但不等于 fully reactive traffic。
claim_ids
BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-C5
evidence_ids
BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-E5
controversy_ids
BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::CONFLICT-10
module_ids
RM-08 · RM-09
claim_ceiling
只能声称在该 benchmark 原生协议内改善 planning score。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
将相同场景移植到具有响应式交通参与者的仿真，并固定 controller 与碰撞口径。
failure_criteria
在 reactive 版本中增益消失、排名反转或出现新的交互碰撞。
odd_benchmark_boundary
不得将 NAVSIM 原生分数直接翻译为真实道路事故率或通用闭环安全。
被过度宣传
id
HYP-02
标题
把更长或更可读的 CoT 当成更强驾驶能力
summary
显式推理文本容易被当成能力展示，但可读性、长度和 QA 准确率都不是动作忠实性的替代指标。ReCogDrive 的结果直接显示加入 CoT 不必然提高规划分数。

BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-C7 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C3 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C4
evidence_ids
BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E7 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E3 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E4
controversy_ids
BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::CONFLICT-15
module_ids
RM-03 · RM-06 · RM-14
claim_ceiling
可说 reasoning format 或自适应路径在特定训练设置中相关于效率或分数；不能说文字解释忠实。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
进行方向明确的 wrong-rationale 与 counterfactual rationale 干预，同时监测动作和隐藏状态。
failure_criteria
解释改变但动作不按语义方向改变，或动作改善完全由额外训练数据/compute 解释。
odd_benchmark_boundary
必须使用不能靠文本先验猜答的视觉交互场景，并独立报告语言指标与 E4/E6 指标。
被过度宣传
id
HYP-03
标题
把世界生成质量等同于规划安全
summary
更低 FID/FVD 或更逼真的未来图像只证明生成侧性质。一个高保真碰撞未来仍可能对应灾难性 policy；规划 utility 必须由动作干预和闭环结果证明。
claim_ids
BATCH-D-VLAWorld::VW-C3 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-C5
evidence_ids
BATCH-D-VLAWorld::VW-E3 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-E5
controversy_ids
BATCH-D-VLAWorld::CONFLICT-19 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::CONFLICT-8
module_ids
RM-05 · RM-06
claim_ceiling
生成指标只能支撑 representation/generation claim；planning claim 上限取决于 E4/E6 证据。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
构造生成质量相近但动作后果相反的 counterfactual future，并测试 policy 对正确/错误 future 的响应。
failure_criteria
生成指标改善而 E4/E6 不改善，或 policy 对错误 future 不敏感。
odd_benchmark_boundary
应在动态交互和遮挡场景评测；静态图像质量不构成道路安全证据。
被过度宣传
id
HYP-04
标题
把 safety/physics reward 宣传为硬安全保证
summary
位置、转角、速度、平滑度和阈值惩罚通常是训练期 soft proxy。它们可能改善平均行为，但不等于形式化可行性、碰撞规避保证或分布外约束满足。
claim_ids
BATCH-C-AutoDrive-R2::ADR2-C4 · BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-C6
evidence_ids
BATCH-C-AutoDrive-R2::ADR2-E4 · BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-E6
controversy_ids
BATCH-C-AutoDrive-R2::CONFLICT-4
module_ids
RM-06 · RM-08 · RM-09
claim_ceiling
可称为 physics-motivated 或 safety-oriented training signal；不能称为硬约束或安全证明。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
在训练分布外曲率、低附着、急制动和控制饱和场景中检查约束违例，并与显式 constrained controller 或 safety shield 对照。
failure_criteria
任何关键硬约束出现非零未处理违例，或 reward 高分与物理不可执行动作共存。
odd_benchmark_boundary
reward 的权重和阈值只对报告的数据、车辆模型和 controller 有效；不得跨车辆动力学外推。
被低估
id
UND-01
标题
E5 controller 是闭环归因的核心变量
summary
轨迹并不是 steering/throttle/brake。PID、tracker、MPC 或安全 shield 可以修正、延迟或放大 E4 误差，因此闭环提升可能主要来自 controller，而非 VLA policy。
claim_ids
BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-C4 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C6
evidence_ids
BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-E4 · BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E6
controversy_ids
module_ids
RM-08 · RM-09
claim_ceiling
已知少数系统明确报告 controller；未报告者只能评价到 E4 或其 benchmark 提供的黑箱执行结果。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
同一 E4 轨迹依次接 PID、纯跟踪器和 MPC，固定车辆动力学与控制频率，测 E6 碰撞、舒适、tracking error 和干预。
failure_criteria
不同 controller 导致的 E6 方差大于 planner 间差异，或 planner 排名随 controller 反转。
odd_benchmark_boundary
结论只适用于指定车辆模型、控制频率、执行延迟与 actuator limits。
被低估
id
UND-02
标题
candidate selector 可能比生成器更决定闭环收益
summary
多候选、diffusion 或 flow 的价值不仅在能生成多样轨迹，还在能否识别可行、安全且有进展的候选。oracle best-of-K 与实际 selector 的差距通常被单个 headline metric 隐藏。
claim_ids
BATCH-A-ORION::OR-C3 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-C4 · BATCH-E-HiST-VLA::HST-C5
evidence_ids
BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E3 · BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E4 · BATCH-E-HiST-VLA::HST-E5 · BATCH-E-HiST-VLA::HST-E6
controversy_ids
BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::CONFLICT-15
module_ids
RM-08 · RM-13
claim_ceiling
可确认候选生成和选择是可分的机制；不能把 best-of-K 能力归因给实际 policy。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
固定候选集合，比较 oracle、学习 selector、规则 selector 与随机 selector，并报告 coverage、calibration、regret 和 E6。
failure_criteria
实际 selector 无法稳定优于随机或规则基线，或候选覆盖提升不转化为 E6 提升。
odd_benchmark_boundary
必须包含候选语义真正分叉的交互场景，普通直路不能检验 selection。
被低估
id
UND-03
标题
视觉 grounding 可靠性比语言流畅度更基础
summary
DriveBench 表明模型在 corrupted、missing-image 或 text-only 条件下仍可能输出可信答案；数据不平衡与指标也会遮蔽 grounding failure。流畅解释因此不能替代视觉依赖性测试。
claim_ids
ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-C3 · ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-C4 · ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-C5
evidence_ids
ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-E3 · ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-E4 · ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-E5
controversy_ids
module_ids
RM-01 · RM-02 · RM-03 · RM-06
claim_ceiling
可证明某些 QA/解释输出存在视觉捷径风险；不能直接推断具体 driving policy 的闭环失效概率。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
为动作任务加入 missing-image、局部遮挡、对象替换、文本冲突与图像-指令反事实对，测 E4 的因果敏感性和置信校准。
failure_criteria
缺失或错误视觉信息时模型仍高置信输出相同动作，或只依赖文本 cue 即达到原分数。
odd_benchmark_boundary
从 QA 迁移到动作任务时必须保留相同视觉因果结构，不能只复用语言评分。
被低估
id
UND-04
标题
artifact 完整性决定结果能否成为公共知识
summary
论文结构、代码、权重、训练数据、reasoning corpus、controller 和评测脚本是不同层级。部分发布足以检查架构，却不足以复现训练收益或部署链。
claim_ids
BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-C5 · BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-C7 · BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-C7

RM-01 · RM-03 · RM-06 · RM-08 · RM-09
claim_ceiling
可分别确认 paper architecture 和已释放 artifact；不能把 repository 存在等同于端到端可复现。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
从空环境按公开说明重建一条最小训练与评测链，记录 data/version/config/checkpoint hash、缺失依赖和数值容差。
failure_criteria
关键数据、reasoning corpus、checkpoint、controller 或评测脚本缺失，导致 headline 结果无法在预注册容差内重现。
odd_benchmark_boundary

RQ-01
研究问题
如何建立动作级 faithful reasoning 的因果检验，而不是依赖文本可读性？
假设
若 visible 或 latent reasoning 是动作中介，定向错误干预应以可预测方式改变 E4/E6，且中介效应可重复。
claim_ids

failure_criteria
中介效应跨 seed 不稳定、方向错误或仅文本输出变化。
odd_benchmark_boundary

成功实验最多证明新排序在预注册场景分布与风险函数下更稳定。
minimum_falsifiable_experiment
预注册遮挡、施工、传感退化、旧导航和高交互切片，报告 CVaR、严重失败率、校准、stop 成功和 progress。
failure_criteria

让独立团队按合同复现三种发布完整度不同的系统，并记录成功率、时间和数值偏差。

结论与研究机会：不要急着选赢家，先把可证伪边界建立起来

1. 这轮研究已经明确了什么

自动驾驶 VLA 到 2026 年中已经不再只是“把 VLM 接到驾驶数据上”的模糊概念。跨 26 个代表系统、240 条规范化主张和 273 个证据窗口，可以稳定识别出一条运行时主链：视觉或多模态观测进入感知编码，经过几何 grounding、时序记忆或 world state，与语言和导航条件共同形成 reasoning state，再经 reasoning-to-action bridge 进入动作表示、轨迹生成器、候选选择器，最后由 controller 转成车辆执行。这个主链对应 RM-01 至 RM-09；multi-expert、scene-adaptive routing、candidate selector、reflection 等 RM-10 至 RM-16 是叠加在主链上的特定机制，而不是可以任意互换的宣传标签。

因此，严格 driving-policy VLA 的最低判据也已经清楚：语言条件必须在系统边界内直接参与 E4，即 waypoint、trajectory、action token 或连续动作的生成。AlphaDrive 的高层 meta-action 仍需要系统外 action model，适合作为边界案例；OpenDriveVLA 的 structured scene/map/agent token 到 waypoint token、AutoVLA 的 physical action vocabulary、ORION 的 planning token 到 generative planner，则给出了不同的动作桥接实例。[BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E9][BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E1][BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E3][BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E2][BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E3] 但“接口存在”只够做结构分类，还不能证明语言被实际使用，更不能证明解释忠实或系统安全。

第二个已经明确的结论是：VLA 的演化不是模块消失，而是接口和训练拓扑被重排。UniDriveVLA 通过 specialist experts 分离理解、感知和动作，DriveVLA-W0 用较小的 Action Expert 承担实时动作生成，LMDrive 和 AutoVLA 则明确把轨迹交给 PID controller。[BATCH-A-UniDriveVLA::UV-E1][BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-E3][BATCH-B-LMDrive::LM-E4][BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E6] 这意味着“端到端”不能被理解为没有 geometry、planner、selector 或 controller，而应被拆成：哪些模块共同训练、哪些模块运行时存在、梯度在哪里停止、E4 到 E5 由谁负责。

第三个明确结论是证据层级有严格上限。离线 L2 只能说明与日志轨迹的接近程度；open-loop planning score 仍然不观察 ego 动作改变世界后的反馈；non-reactive replay 与 pseudo-simulation 也不能自动升级成 fully reactive closed loop。DriveWorld-VLA 的材料已经明确区分 NAVSIM v1 的 non-reactive 与 v2 的 pseudo-simulation，ORION 在 Bench2Drive 与 nuScenes open-loop 的相对表现也提示单一 benchmark 强项不能扩写成普适优势。[BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-E5][BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E5][BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E8] 所有能力或安全结论都必须绑定 simulator/version、agent reactiveness、sensor、horizon、controller、metric denominator、场景抽样和 ODD。

2. 仍然不知道什么

最重要的未知不是“下一个 backbone 会多大”，而是中间机制是否真的被动作使用。显式 CoT 尤其如此。AutoVLA 的证据支持自适应 fast/slow reasoning 与 reward、效率之间存在设置内关系；ReCogDrive 却显示加入显式 CoT 后 NAVSIM PDMS 下降 0.1。[BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E3][BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E4][BATCH-C-ReCogDrive::RC-E7] 这两者并不矛盾，它们共同说明“输出了 reasoning”与“reasoning 是忠实动作中介”是两件事。没有 correct/wrong/counterfactual rationale 的定向干预，没有对 E4 与 E6 的中介分析，就不能把可读解释当作 policy 的真实理由。

world model 也处在相似状态。future-image supervision、action-conditioned BEV imagination、dynamics latent 和 geometry teacher 都可能改善 representation，但生成质量与 planning utility 不是同一个变量。VLAWorld 明确提醒高保真地生成碰撞未来并不会让 policy 自动安全；DriveVLA-W0 的规模与 world-model ablation 也只能支持其内部设置，不能外推为普适 scaling law。[BATCH-D-VLAWorld::VW-E3][BATCH-A-DriveVLA-W0::W0-E5] 真正缺失的是 matched-capacity temporal baseline，以及 correct、wrong、time-shifted、zero latent 注入对 E4 和 reactive E6 的影响。

实时性与部署证据同样不完整。DriveVLM、ORION 和 VLGA 都把大模型延迟视为约束，Reasoning-VLA 则在 H200/vLLM 设置下报告快速并行轨迹生成。[BATCH-B-DriveVLM::DV-E6][BATCH-A-ORION::OR-E6][BATCH-E-VLGA::VLG-E12][BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-E10] 这些数值缺少统一的硬件、precision、batch、view count、语言路径、采样步数、预后处理、selector 与 controller 合同，因而不能形成可信横排。更不能把数据中心 GPU 的单次 forward 时间直接写成车端实时闭环能力。

最后，真实道路安全仍是最大证据缺口。多数系统的最强证据停留在 open-loop、non-reactive、pseudo-sim 或有限 reactive simulator。缺失的不是一个更漂亮的平均分，而是按暴露量和场景类型报告的碰撞、near-miss、干预、minimum-risk stop、恢复时间、传感退化、软件版本、fallback 和独立审计。没有这些字段，量产、公开道路和通用安全结论都必须保留为未知。

3. 哪些主张被过度宣传

第一类过度宣传是术语升级：把 non-reactive 或 pseudo-simulation 写成完整闭环，把一次演示写成真实道路能力，把 repository 存在写成端到端可复现。DriveWorld-VLA 的 closed-loop 术语争议、AutoVLA reasoning corpus 未完整释放、OpenDriveVLA training/checkpoint 支持不完整，都要求把“论文结构确认”“代码存在”“权重可用”“训练可复现”“部署链可复现”分开报告。[BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-E5][BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E5][BATCH-A-OpenDriveVLA::OD-E7]

第二类是机制名字替代因果证据。CoT 更长不等于驾驶更强；diffusion 或 flow 能生成多候选不等于能选中安全候选；future image 更清晰不等于规划更安全；reward 名称含 safety 或 physics 也不等于硬约束。AutoDrive-R2 的 position、steering、velocity、smoothness 项和 Reasoning-VLA 的 steering/acceleration 阈值，本质上都是训练期 soft proxy。[BATCH-C-AutoDrive-R2::ADR2-E4][BATCH-C-Reasoning-VLA::RVL-E6] 它们可以被称为 physics-motivated 或 safety-oriented，但不能被写成形式化可行性保证。

第三类是 universal SOTA 叙事。ORION 在 Bench2Drive 的强结果不能覆盖其 nuScenes open-loop 相对表现；UniDriveVLA 的 broad SOTA 也应收缩到特定 metric、sensor 和 comparison set。跨论文排行榜只有在 data split、输入、backbone、horizon、controller、metric implementation 与环境交互强度一致时才有意义。否则最诚实的产出不是一个总榜，而是一张“完全可比、条件可比、方向参考、不可比”的资格表。

4. 哪些问题被低估

最被低估的是 E5 controller。轨迹不是 steering、throttle 和 brake；PID、tracker、MPC 或 safety shield 可以修正、延迟或放大 E4 的误差。如果更换 controller 后 E6 排名反转，那么此前被归给 VLA planner 的收益至少部分属于执行层。controller-aware attribution 应成为所有闭环论文的标准 factorial experiment，而不是附录中的实现细节。

第二个被低估的是 candidate selector。多模态生成的真正价值不是 oracle 能在 K 条轨迹里找到一条好轨迹，而是实际 selector 能否在时延预算内稳定选中它。应同时报告 candidate coverage、oracle best-of-K、实际 selector、规则 selector、随机 selector、calibration 与 regret。若 coverage 提升没有转化为 E6 提升，研究结论应指向 selector 瓶颈，而不是继续扩大生成器。

第三个被低估的是 grounding 可靠性。DriveBench 表明模型可能在 corrupted、missing-image 和 text-only 条件下依靠常识或文本 cue 输出可信答案，数据不平衡与指标又会掩盖失败。[ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-E3][ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-E4][ANCHOR-DriveBench::DB-E5] 对 driving policy 来说，必须把这一审计推进到动作层：对象替换、局部遮挡、空间移动、图像-指令冲突和 missing-image 应引起方向正确的 E4 变化与置信下降。语言流畅度不能替代视觉因果依赖。

第四个被低估的是负结果和 unknown。删除 memory 后只有遮挡切片退化，比“平均分下降”更能界定机制；wrong rationale 不影响动作，应撤回 visible-CoT faithfulness，而不是武断地断言系统没有 latent reasoning；world metric 变化但 E4 不变，应保留 representation claim、撤回 planning claim。unknown 也应分成 blocker、decision-sensitive 与 nice-to-have，成为下一次实验的输入，而不是被猜测填满。

5. 六个适合初学者的项目

初学者最值得先做的不是复现一个最大模型，而是建立审计能力。第一，完成十个匿名系统的 E0-E6 接口卡，记录 shape、坐标、时间、ownership、unknown 和证据等级。第二，做 benchmark 可比性审计器，能拒绝把 non-reactive 与 reactive、不同 sensor/controller 或不同分母的结果排在同一榜。第三，做 delete、replace、shuffle、irrelevant 四格语言干预，检验语言是否定向改变 E4。第四，对同一批轨迹进行 PID、tracker、MPC controller swap，分离 planner 与执行层。第五，建立候选覆盖与 selector regret 小基准，比较 oracle、学习、规则和随机 selector。第六，从干净环境完成一条公开 artifact 的最小复现链，记录 data/config/code/checkpoint hash 与数值容差。

这六个项目的共同要求是先写失败判据。例如语言相反替换没有方向性效果，就把结论降为结构接入；controller 差异大于 planner 差异，就停止声称纯 planner 优势；实际 selector 不优于规则基线，就撤回“多候选已转化为闭环能力”；缺少 reasoning corpus 或评测脚本，就把 artifact 状态写成 partial。能主动收缩主张，比勉强得到一个正结果更接近真实研究。

6. 八个研究级开放问题

未来最值得投入的八个问题是：一，建立动作级 faithful reasoning 的定向因果检验；二，区分 world supervision、runtime latent 与显式 rollout 对 planning 的独立贡献；三，在 matched compute 下拆分 generator coverage 与 selector 能力；四，把 planner-controller interaction 纳入闭环因果归因；五，建立统一的 latency-energy-memory 端到端 Pareto 基准；六，用 tail risk、calibrated fallback 与 minimum-risk stop 重构安全排序；七，把 grounding 可靠性从 QA 推进到动作后果；八，建立跨版本、跨团队可复现的 artifact 与 benchmark 合同。

这些问题必须以最小可证伪实验起步，而不是以“做一个更大的系统”为起点。最小不等于样本少，而是一次只改变一个决定性变量，冻结 data split、seed、horizon、controller、environment 和 metric，预注册主指标、失败阈值与停止规则，并保存 run ID、config、code、data、checkpoint 和结果 hash。只有当 representation correlation、机制干预、E4 变化、reactive E6 和 ODD 外推证据逐级成立时，claim ceiling 才能上升。

最终结论不是某条路线已经胜出。现有证据支持的是一个更克制、也更有生产力的判断：VLA 正在把开放语义、空间 grounding、时序状态和分布式动作生成接入同一驾驶决策链，但最关键的科学缺口仍位于接口因果、选择机制、controller 归因、响应式闭环、尾部安全和可复现 artifact。下一阶段真正有价值的工作，不是再增加一个名称，而是让每个名称对应一个可干预模块、一个明确的 claim ceiling、一个会失败的实验，以及一个不能被越过的 ODD 边界。

证据追溯（Evidence Trace）

本节展示全部 240 条规范化主张，并把每条主张连接到原始证据窗口、来源 ID、状态和置信度；273 个证据窗口同时保存在机器可枚举注册表中。

证据追溯的作用不是堆引用，而是限制结论强度：论文自报、官方工件、独立评测、来源冲突、合理推断和未知必须可区分；任何跨系统结论都应能沿这条链回到原始来源窗口。

AlphaDriveAlphaDrive is a Qwen2VL-based high-level planning VLM adapted with reasoning and reinforcement learning.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C1 · supported · high

AlphaDrive is a VLM designed for autonomous driving planning. Unlike previous approaches that rely solely on SFT, we explore the incorporation of RL and reasoning techniques to better align with the unique characteristics of driving planning: (1) the varying importance of different driving behaviors; (2) the existence of multiple feasible solutions; and (3) the scarcity of readily available reasoning data for planning decisions.

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E1 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML

Training. We use Qwen2VL-2B [41] as the base model. Qwen2VL is currently one of the best-performing open-source models, and it offers a smaller 2B version that better meets the latency requirements for autonomous driving. Additionally, Qwen2VL provides better support for RL. The model’s inputs include a front-view image and a planning prompt, which contains the vehicle’s current speed and navigation information. The navigation data, consistent with real-world driving, is obtained from sparse navigation points via AMap (similar to Google Maps) and is converted into text form for inclusion in the prompt, such as “Go straight for 100m, then turn right”. Training is conducted using 16 NVIDIA A800 GPUs.

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E5 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AlphaDriveIts GRPO reward combines planning accuracy, safety-oriented action weighting, output diversity, and format compliance.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C2 · supported · high

AlphaDrive introduces four GRPO rewards tailored for planning. The first is the planning accuracy reward, which evaluates the consistency between the model’s planning actions and the ground truth actions. The second is the action-weighted reward, which assigns different weights to various actions based on their importance to safety. For instance, actions such as braking and steering are critical for safety, so weighting them accordingly helps the model achieve better performance in planning key actions. The third is the planning diversity reward, which encourages the model to generate multiple diverse solutions. This prevents mode collapse and enhances overall planning performance. The last one is the planning format reward, where we define a specific output format and encourage the model to follow it. This ensures more structured outputs and contributes to more stable training.

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E2 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AlphaDrivePlanning reasoning is distilled from GPT-4o through SFT before full-data GRPO training.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C3 · supported · high

In addition to RL, we propose a planning reasoning technique. Our approach employs a two-stage training strategy based on knowledge distillation, integrating SFT and RL. In the first stage, we leverage a large model, such as GPT-4o, to generate a small yet high-quality dataset containing planning reasoning processes derived from real driving actions. This dataset is then used to fine-tune our model via SFT, effectively distilling knowledge from the large model. In the second stage, we further refine the model using RL. Introducing the SFT stage as a warm-up step effectively mitigates hallucinations and instability commonly observed in the early stages of reinforcement learning, while also enhancing planning performance.

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E3 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AlphaDriveThe sole reported benchmark is a 110k/10k MetaAD train-validation split with a 30k reasoning subset.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C4 · supported · high

Dataset. We adopt MetaAD, a large-scale real-world driving dataset, as our training and evaluation benchmark. This dataset consists of a total of 120k driving clips, each lasting three seconds. MetaAD is a high-quality dataset specifically designed for planning, supporting multi-sensor data and perception annotations. Furthermore, it maintains a well-balanced distribution across various driving environments and planning actions. The dataset is divided into 110k clips for training and 10k clips for validation. As for reasoning, we sample 30k data from the training dataset to generate the planning reasoning process. All reported results are obtained by training on the training set and evaluating on the validation set.

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E4 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AlphaDriveThe reported model uses Qwen2VL-2B with front-view, speed, and navigation inputs and is trained on 16 A800 GPUs.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C5 · supported · high

Training. We use Qwen2VL-2B [41] as the base model. Qwen2VL is currently one of the best-performing open-source models, and it offers a smaller 2B version that better meets the latency requirements for autonomous driving. Additionally, Qwen2VL provides better support for RL. The model’s inputs include a front-view image and a planning prompt, which contains the vehicle’s current speed and navigation information. The navigation data, consistent with real-world driving, is obtained from sparse navigation points via AMap (similar to Google Maps) and is converted into text form for inclusion in the prompt, such as “Go straight for 100m, then turn right”. Training is conducted using 16 NVIDIA A800 GPUs.

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E5 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AlphaDriveAlphaDrive reports 77.12% planning accuracy and a 25.5% relative gain over the strongest listed SFT baseline.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C6 · supported · high

Tab. 1 presents the performance of AlphaDrive in high-level planning. The first four rows show the results obtained by directly evaluating the corresponding pretrained models. It can be observed that, while these models demonstrate stronger general capabilities, their performance in planning is suboptimal, highlighting the need for further training with driving data. The subsequent five rows display the results of models fine-tuned on the MetaAD dataset. As shown, AlphaDrive significantly outperforms the other models. Compared to Qwen2VL-7B, the second-best performing model after AlphaDrive, the planning accuracy significantly improves by 25.5%. There is a noticeable enhancement in key decisions such as steering and acceleration/deceleration. Additionally, the quality of planning reasoning is the best among all models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed two-stage RL training

BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E6 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AlphaDriveReasoning SFT warm-up followed by RL produces stronger reasoning metrics than SFT-only or RL-only training.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C7 · supported · high

Reasoning Training Strategies. The ablation study of the reasoning training strategies is shown in Tab. 3. As observed, introducing planning reasoning under different training strategies effectively enhances model performance. Notably, the improvement is especially significant for complex actions such as acceleration and deceleration, demonstrating that reasoning can greatly enhance decision-making in complex scenarios. Furthermore, the model trained exclusively with RL performs worse in reasoning compared to the model trained with SFT. We attribute this to the limited parameter size of smaller models, which results in insufficient perception and reasoning capabilities. Therefore, incorporating SFT as a warm-up phase and using knowledge distillation to learn the reasoning process from a larger model can effectively address this issue. By combining SFT and RL, the model achieves the best

AlphaDriveAlphaDrive lacks richer maneuvers and depends on imperfect pseudo-labeled reasoning.

主张：BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-C8 · supported · high

Currently, due to a lack of rich data annotation, AlphaDrive is still unable to output more complex driving behaviors such as lane changes or nudges. Additionally, the current planning reasoning data come from pseudo-labels generated by large models based on ground-truth driving actions, which still suffer from inaccurate perception and a failure to capture key factors. Therefore, further systematic validation is required to improve data quality and verify the performance upper bound of AlphaDrive.

In autonomous driving, recent end-to-end models have greatly improved planning performance but still struggle with long-tailed problems due to limited common sense and reasoning abilities. BATCH-C-AlphaDrive::ALD-E10 · ARXIV-2503.07608-HTML
AnchorVLAAnchorVLA factorizes planning into a discrete trajectory-pattern anchor decision and continuous residual execution.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C1 · supported · high

Given the current scene observation, navigation information, and language instruction as the context 𝑥 , the model predicts a future trajectory 𝜏 . Instead of modeling the trajectory distribution 𝑝 ​ ( 𝜏 ∣ 𝑥 ) as a monolithic continuous prediction problem, AnchorVLA decomposes trajectory generation into two stages: behavior-level decision making over trajectory-pattern anchors and fine-grained continuous execution within the selected anchor-defined residual space.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E1 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML

This decomposition captures the core idea of AnchorVLA: the trajectory-pattern anchor is not merely an additional condition for trajectory generation, but an explicit decision interface that defines the residual coordinate system and the local behavior generation space. In this way, high-level VLA reasoning first selects behavior-level anchors, and low-level continuous generation is then performed within the corresponding anchor-defined residual spaces. The following sections introduce how trajectory-pattern anchors are predicted under DAAR and how executable continuous trajectories are generated using Decision-Anchored Residual Flow (DARF).

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E2 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML
AnchorVLADAAR represents each behavior-level decision as one complete local motion-pattern anchor rather than a sequence of coordinate tokens.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C2 · supported · high

Decision-as-Anchor Representation (DAAR) maps high-level VLA reasoning into a compact trajectory-pattern anchor space. Instead of asking the LLM to autoregressively generate low-level coordinate or waypoint tokens, DAAR represents each behavior-level driving decision with a trajectory-pattern anchor, where each anchor corresponds to a complete local motion pattern. Therefore, anchor prediction can be formulated as estimating a distribution over the trajectory-pattern anchor space.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E3 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML
AnchorVLABoth query-based and autoregressive anchor decisions share soft nearest-anchor supervision, while autoregressive inference is restricted to a compact anchor-token vocabulary.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C3 · supported · high

Instead of using a single hard label, we construct a soft anchor target over the nearest anchor set 𝒩 , assigning larger weights to anchors closer to the ground-truth trajectory to preserve local continuity in the trajectory-pattern space. The soft target is defined as

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E4 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML

Thus, autoregressive modeling reuses the LLM next-token prediction interface while restricting the output space to the compact anchor-token vocabulary and optimizing with the soft anchor target. During inference, the LLM predicts anchor-token logits conditioned on the context and the control token, which are converted into trajectory-pattern anchor probabilities through the fixed anchor-token mapping.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E5 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML
AnchorVLADARF performs flow matching in the coordinate system of each selected anchor and preserves multiple candidate behaviors until confidence-based selection.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C4 · supported · high

Given the ground-truth trajectory 𝜏 gt , we construct the target residual for each candidate anchor as 𝑟 gt 𝑘 = 𝜏 gt − 𝑎 𝑘 . Compared with full-flow modeling in the complete trajectory coordinate space, i.e., 𝑧 𝑡 full = ( 1 − 𝑡 ) ​ 𝜖 + 𝑡 ​ 𝜏 gt , DARF performs flow matching in a residual coordinate system centered at the selected anchor. When the selected anchor provides a good trajectory reference, the residual target is typically more compact than the full trajectory target, which makes limited-step flow integration easier in practice.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E6 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML

Through this design, DARF generates continuous trajectories for multiple candidate trajectory-pattern anchors and selects the final output using confidence scores, thereby maintaining multiple high-level behavior hypotheses before final trajectory selection.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E7 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML
AnchorVLAThe anchor-decision model is trained first; then the VLA backbone is frozen while DARF learns residual flow and confidence.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C5 · supported · high

Training. We train the model in two stages. First, we train the anchor-decision model under DAAR to predict trajectory-pattern anchors for 15 epochs on 8 NVIDIA A100 GPUs with batch size 16 . Then, we freeze the VLA backbone and train DARF for residual-based continuous trajectory generation for 15 epochs on 4 NVIDIA A100 GPUs with batch size 32 .

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E8 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML

AnchorVLAAblations favor autoregressive anchor decisions over query decisions and anchor-residual flow over deterministic residual or full-trajectory flow.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E11 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML

Our Decision-Anchored Residual Flow achieves 89.92 DS and 77.28 SR, outperforming both variants under the same number of flow steps. This suggests that, in our setting, modeling continuous residuals in the anchor-defined residual space is more effective. Since the anchor 𝑎 𝑘 already provides a coarse trajectory reference, the residual target 𝑟 gt 𝑘 = 𝜏 gt − 𝑎 𝑘 is typically more compact than the full trajectory target 𝜏 gt , which tends to shorten the transport path and make limited-step generation easier.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E12 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML
AnchorVLAIncorrect anchor selection propagates into DARF because residual generation remains local to the chosen anchor.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C8 · supported · high

Figure 5 presents representative failure cases of AnchorVLA. The main issue is error propagation caused by incorrect trajectory-pattern anchor predictions. Since DARF generates trajectories in the anchor-defined residual space, it tends to refine the selected behavior rather than override the behavior specified by the selected anchor. Thus, when the predicted anchor is off-route or inconsistent with the language instruction, the final trajectory may also follow an undesired behavior pattern. In other cases, the predicted anchor is roughly correct, but DARF may introduce local geometric deviations during residual refinement.

BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-E13 · ARXIV-2607.03182-HTML
AnchorVLAThe appendix specifies separate velocity and confidence branches, separating residual generation from candidate-anchor compatibility scoring.

主张：BATCH-E-AnchorVLA::ANC-C9 · supported · high

In this way, the DARF decoder adopts a decoupled two-branch design. The velocity branch focuses on fine-grained residual generation in the anchor-defined residual space, while the confidence branch evaluates candidate-anchor compatibility with the driving context for final trajectory selection.

Despite recent progress, existing VLA models face two critical limitations in autonomous driving, as illustrated in Fig.˜2. 1) Physically-infeasible or complex structure for action generation. Some models generate textual actions or waypoints directly using VLMs [shao2024lmdrive, renz2025simlingo, hwang2024emma], but these outputs can be physically infeasible and suffer from mode collapse. BATCH-A-AutoVLA::AV-E6 · ARXIV-2506.13757-HTML
ColaVLAColaVLA uses two VLM passes for latent reasoning and one pass for hierarchical parallel trajectory planning.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C1 · supported · high

First, the cognitive latent reasoner efficiently accomplishes scene understanding and the final meta-action decision through two forward passes. Specifically, during the first forward pass, the reasoner constructs a multimodal input sequence comprising a fixed driving prompt, multi-view visual images, and ego status, and passes it through a VLM to obtain unified tokens that have undergone complete contextual interaction. However, the visual tokens contain substantial redundant information irrelevant to the driving decision. To extract decision-relevant information from the scene, we introduce an ego-adaptive modulation to align these tokens with the instantaneous vehicle state, after which a lightweight router scores and selects the top- 𝐾 safety-critical vision tokens. Subsequently, for the second forward pass, we concatenate the selected context with learnable meta queries as input, e

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E1 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAAn ego-conditioned differentiable router retains the top-K safety-critical visual tokens.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C2 · supported · high

During training, the selection is made differentiable using a Gumbel–Softmax relaxation to form a 𝐾 -hot mask, while at inference time the top- 𝐾 tokens are retained directly. The resulting compact set 𝐐 ∗ ∈ ℝ 𝐾 × 𝐷 effectively captures the most safety-relevant visual cues, e.g. lanes, nearby vehicles, pedestrians, and traffic lights, serving as an efficient information bottleneck for the subsequent latent reasoning stage.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E2 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAMeta queries classify several probable maneuver strategies and provide action-bank priors to the planner.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C3 · supported · high

Strategic Decision Synthesis. The meta-query embeddings 𝐐 M are adapted to the ego state through FiLM modulation and cross-attend to the driving-critical visual tokens 𝐐 ∗ , followed by a self-attention layer over the meta tokens. A shared two-layer MLP then maps each refined meta token to a maneuver logit, trained with a focal loss that emphasizes hard and safety-critical cases. By constraining the reasoning space to 𝐶 meta-action tokens, the process achieves entropy reduction and produces multiple probable driving strategies, which form structured priors for subsequent prediction.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E3 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAThe hybrid mask allows global context and same-scale attention while preventing access to future finer scales.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C4 · supported · high

Causality-Preserving Hybrid Attention. We design a hybrid attention mask ℳ to regulate information flow between the pruned context and multi-scale trajectory tokens as shown in Fig. 3. It satisfies three principles: (i) bidirectional interaction within each category, enabling context tokens and same-scale trajectory tokens to maintain local consistency; (ii) global context aggregation, allowing every trajectory token to attend to all context tokens; and (iii) causal preservation, where tokens at scale can only access those from the preceding coarser scale − 1 , preventing information leakage from future finer scales.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E4 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAConfidence-guided decoding trains only the closest trajectory hypothesis with direct regression while preserving multiple modes.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C5 · supported · high

Confidence-Guided Parallel Decoding. In the final decoding stage, the planner simultaneously processes multiple candidate driving strategies, each producing a set of latent trajectory targets. Two lightweight MLP heads are applied independently to estimate the confidence score and to regress the corresponding multi-scale trajectory for each hypothesis. During training, a one-hot supervision signal is assigned based on the distance between each predicted trajectory and the ground-truth, where only the closest hypothesis receives direct regression supervision. This confidence-guided mechanism enables the model to prioritize the most reliable trajectory while preserving diversity across hypotheses. The parallel decoding further processes all candidates within a single forward pass, ensuring high efficiency and enhanced generalization by effectively preventing mode collapse.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E5 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLATraining adapts the VLM on OmniDrive QA before integrating and jointly fine-tuning the action planner.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C6 · supported · high

We use a multi-stage training strategy. In the first stage, our VLM is pretrained on QA pairs from OmniDrive-nuScenes, where LoRA layers [19] are applied to prompt and adapt the VLM efficiently for perception–planning alignment. In the second stage, we integrate our action-based planner and perform joint fine-tuning. Within the VLM, we only update the LoRA parameters to retain pretrained knowledge while improving decision capability. Training is conducted with the AdamW optimizer [32] with Cosine Annealing [33], using a weight decay of 1 × 10 − 4 and an initial learning rate of 1 × 10 − 4 . Additional details and hyperparameters are provided in the supplementary material.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E10 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAThe final implementation is six-camera-only despite the general multimodal formulation.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C10 · supported · high

Architecture. Our implementation is built on the LLaVA v1.5 framework [29], which uses LLaMA-7B as the language backbone and introduces LoRA adapters [19] for efficient adaptation. The image encoder follows EVA-02-L [14] instantiated as an EVAViT backbone with 24 transformer layers, hidden dimension 1024 and window size 16, initialized from publicly available weights. On top of the image backbone, we adopt the SQ-Former architecture [8] for multi-view visual reasoning and use perception task heads. We use 900 object queries and 300 lane queries by default, consistent with our main experiments. The overall system is implemented in a single camera-only configuration with 6 multi-view images per frame. And point-cloud-style BEV parameters (e.g., point cloud range and voxel size) are used only for defining the spatial grid for 3D perception heads.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E11 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAFinal-stage training uses 16 H20 GPUs for ten nuScenes epochs with AdamW at 1e-4.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C11 · supported · high

Optimization and training schedule. Unless otherwise specified, all stages are trained on 16 NVIDIA H20 GPUs with a per-GPU batch size of 2 (effective batch size 32). We use the AdamW optimizer [32] with initial learning rate 1 × 10 − 4 , weight decay 1 × 10 − 4 , and ( 𝛽 1 , 𝛽 2 ) = ( 0.9 , 0.999 ) . The learning rate is scheduled by Cosine Annealing [33] with linear warmup for the first 500 iterations and a minimum learning-rate ratio of 10 − 3 . We train for 10 epochs on nuScenes dataset in the final stage, using mixed-precision (FP16) with dynamic loss scaling and gradient clipping at a global norm of 10.

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E12 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
ColaVLAThe meta-action bank contains eight path-speed classes derived from trajectories and CAN bus signals.

主张：BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-C12 · supported · high

To support cognitive latent reasoning, we pre-compute a discrete meta-action label for each nuScenes sample and use it to index the action bank. Each meta-action is defined by combining a path pattern with a speed pattern derived jointly from the future ego trajectory and CAN bus signals. In our final setup, this yields eight meta-action classes: four straight-driving modes (stopping, constant-speed cruising, accelerating, and decelerating along a roughly straight path) and four turning or lane-change modes (large left turns or U-turns, large right turns or U-turns, small left turns or lane changes, and small right turns or lane changes). Our implementation broadly follows the trajectory categorization procedure used in Senna [23].

BATCH-C-ColaVLA::COL-E14 · ARXIV-2512.22939-HTML
CoWorld-VLACoWorld-VLA encodes semantic, geometric, dynamic, and ego-trajectory knowledge as four planner-accessible expert-token groups.

主张：BATCH-D-CoWorld-VLA::CW-C1 · supported · high

These limitations point to a central bottleneck: current VLA systems lack a mechanism for turning complementary world knowledge into planning-oriented latent states. To address this, we propose CoWorld-VLA, a multi-expert world reasoning framework for autonomous driving (Figure 1(d)). CoWorld-VLA introduces four specialized expert tokens in the VLM latent space to form a planning-oriented Latent CoT: semantic interaction token for high-level intentions and interactions, guided by JEPA-style representations [1, 2], the geometric structure token for road layouts and spatial constraints, using VGGT features [65], dynamic evolution token for future scene evolution, supervised by the generative world model Wan [64], and ego-trajectory tokens that connect world reasoning with behavioral objectives through trajectory-level supervision. Together, these tokens condense complementary world knowled

BATCH-D-CoWorld-VLA::CW-E1 · ARXIV-2605.10426-HTML
CoWorld-VLAA Hierarchical Multi-Expert Fusion diffusion planner denoises continuous trajectories conditioned directly on expert tokens and scene context.

主张：BATCH-D-CoWorld-VLA::CW-C2 · supported · high

Given the multi-expert tokens learned in Sec. 3.3, CoWorld-VLA generates continuous ego trajectories with a Hierarchical Multi-Expert Fusion (HMEF) planner. HMEF performs conditional denoising in the normalized action space, allowing expert tokens to directly guide trajectory generation.

BATCH-D-CoWorld-VLA::CW-E2 · ARXIV-2605.10426-HTML
CoWorld-VLAThe system trains a Wan2.2-5B video DiT, a Qwen3-VL-2B multi-expert representation model, and a frozen-VLM action expert in three stages.

主张：BATCH-D-CoWorld-VLA::CW-C3 · supported · high

CoWorld-VLA consists of a video diffusion Transformer (Wan2.2-5B [64]), a VLM (Qwen3-VL-2B [3]), and an action expert network. We adopt a three-stage training strategy: video DiT pretraining on NuPlan for future video generation, VLM fine-tuning with multi-expert supervision on NAVSIM v1, and action expert training with the VLM frozen. During inference, 20 sampling steps are used for video generation and 10 for trajectory planning. 𝑤 dyn =1.0, 𝑤 sem =0.1, 𝑤 geo =0.1, and 𝑤 traj =1.0. More details are shown in the appendix.

However, existing reasoning mechanisms still struggle to provide planning-oriented intermediate representations: textual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) fails to preserve continuous spatiotemporal structure, while latent world reasoning remains difficult to use as a direct condition for action generation. BATCH-B-DriveVLM::DV-E6 · ARXIV-2402.12289-HTML
DriveWorld-VLADriveWorld-VLA uses three progressive stages to align joint representation learning, action-conditioned controllability, and reward-guided action refinement.

主张：BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-C1 · supported · high

DriveWorld-VLA is designed to tightly integrate VLA model with World Model in a unified architecture that supports both multi-modal reasoning and prospective imagination. To progressively align representation learning, action controllability, and consequence-aware decision making, we adopt a three-stage training paradigm. Each stage incrementally unlocks a key capability of the world model, while ensuring stable joint optimization with the VLA. Specifically, the training process is organized into three sequential stages: VLA & WM Joint Training, Action Controllability Fine-Tuning and Guided Evaluation & Refinement. Figure 2 shows the pipeline of DriveWorld-VLA.

BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-E1 · ARXIV-2602.06521-HTML
DriveWorld-VLAThe model tokenizes multi-view images, text, historical actions, and BEV features before processing them with an InternVL-based VLM.

主张：BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-C2 · supported · high

DriveWorld-VLA supports multi-modal inputs, including multi-view images ℐ 𝑡 , textual prompts 𝒯 𝑡 , historical actions 𝒜 𝑡 − 1 , and BEV representations ℬ 𝑡 . All modalities are independently tokenized before being fed into the Vision-Language Model (VLM). Image and text tokenization follow InternVL (Zhu et al., 2025), while dedicated tokenizers are introduced for BEV features and historical actions. BEV features ℬ 𝑡 ∈ ℝ 𝐻 × 𝑊 × 𝐶 are extracted by BEVFormer (Li et al., 2024c), flattened spatially, and projected into the VLM embedding space as BEV tokens. Historical ego actions are serialized into natural language prompts and concatenated with textual instructions, then encoded using the same text tokenizer as InternVL.

DriveWorld-VLAPredicted trajectories condition a DiT denoising branch to produce action-conditioned future BEV imagination used in the refinement loop.

主张：BATCH-D-DriveWorld-VLA::DW-C3 · supported · high

This stage aims to establish a closed-loop interaction between action prediction and future imagination based on the highly shared representation ℋ 𝑡 between the VLA and the world model. DriveWorld-VLA is required not only to predict actions but also to effectively imagine the corresponding future outcomes induced by those actions, and to evaluate and refine actions according to the imagined future states. Given the ℬ 𝑡 , ℋ 𝑡 and 𝒜 𝑡 − 1 , DriveWorld-VLA first predicts future actions 𝒜 𝑡 + Δ ​ 𝑡 ′ and generates future imagination ℬ 𝑡 + Δ ​ 𝑡 via the first denoising branch. The predicted actions are subsequently used to condition the second denoising branch, where Euler-based sampling is performed to produce action-conditioned future imagination ℬ 𝑡 + Δ ​ 𝑡 ′ :

Implementation. For NAVSIM, we concatenate the left-front view, front view, and right-front view in the order to form a 256 × 1024 composite image as the model input. We use ResNet-34 (He et al., 2016) as the BEV encoder. Training is performed with the AdamW optimizer using an initial learning rate of 1e-4 and a batch size of 16. Each stage is trained for 20 epochs on 8 NVIDIA H20 GPUs, with a total training time of approximately 120 hours. For the nuScenes open-loop evaluation, the 6-view input images are resized to 640 × 384. We adopt a Swin-T (Liu et al., 2021) backbone initialized with pretrained weights and follow BEV-Planner (Li et al., 2024d) to encode the BEV feature map.
